            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setStatusFilter(statusFilter === 'completed' ? 'all' : 'completed')}
              className={`cursor-pointer rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                statusFilter === 'completed'
                  ? 'border-emerald-400/30 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300'
                  : 'border-white/5 bg-white/5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
              title="Filtrar completadas"
            >
              Completadas
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setStatusFilter(statusFilter === 'critical' ? 'all' : 'critical')}
              className={`cursor-pointer rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                statusFilter === 'critical'
                  ? 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300'
                  : 'border-white/5 bg-white/5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
              title="Filtrar ruta crítica"
            >
              Ruta Crítica
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =========================================================================
          VIEW 1: TASK LIST & INTERACTIVE PROGRESS CONTROLS
          ========================================================================= */}
      {viewMode === 'tasks' && (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-900/80 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 bg-slate-950/60 p-4">
            <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase">
              Listado de Actividades Programadas - Registra el Avance en Tiempo Real
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {filteredTasks.length} de {projectTasks.length} tareas
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="divide-y divide-white/5">
            {filteredTasks.length === 0 && (
              <p className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-400">
                No hay tareas para mostrar. Ajusta los filtros o crea la primera actividad.
              </p>
            )}
            {filteredTasks.map((t) => {
              const isCompleted = t.completed || t.progress >= 100;
              const flowCheck = validateTaskWorkflowFlow(t, projectTasks);

              return (
                <div
                  key={t.id}
                  className={`flex flex-col items-start justify-between gap-5 p-5 transition-all hover:bg-white/[0.02] xl:flex-row xl:items-center ${
                    isCompleted ? 'bg-emerald-950/10' : ''
                  }`}
                >
                  {/* Left: Task Details */}
                  <div className="min-w-[280px] flex-1 space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="rounded-full border border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/15 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider text-sky-300 uppercase">
                        {t.phase}
                      </span>

                      {t.criticalPath && (
                        <span className="rounded-full border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/15 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider text-rose-300 uppercase">
                          Ruta Crítica
                        </span>
                      )}

                      {t.weightPercent && (
                        <span className="rounded border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-amber-300">
                          Ponderación: {t.weightPercent}%
                        </span>
                      )}

                      {flowCheck.isBlocked && !isCompleted && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded border border-amber-400/30 bg-amber-500/15 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300">
                          <AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3 text-amber-400" />
                          Predecesora Requerida
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      {/* Checkbox trigger button */}
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleToggleTaskCompleted(t.id)}
                        className={`flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-lg border transition ${
                          isCompleted
                            ? 'border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(16,185,129,0.5)]'
                            : 'border-white/20 bg-slate-800 text-transparent hover:border-sky-400'
                        }`}
                        title={
                          isCompleted ? 'Marcar como pendiente' : 'Marcar como completada al 100%'
                        }
                      >
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 fill-current stroke-slate-950" />
                      </button>

                      <h4
                        className={`text-sm font-bold tracking-tight transition ${
                          isCompleted ? 'text-slate-400 line-through' : 'text-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {t.name}
                      </h4>
                    </div>

                    {t.notes && <p className="line-clamp-1 text-xs text-slate-400">{t.notes}</p>}

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-slate-400">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                        {t.startDate} al {t.endDate}
                      </span>
                      {t.assignedTo && (
                        <span className="text-slate-300">
                          Responsable: <strong className="text-white">{t.assignedTo}</strong>
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Right: Interactive Slider & Quick Progress Buttons */}
                  <div className="flex w-full shrink-0 flex-col items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-white/5 bg-white/[0.02] p-3 sm:flex-row xl:w-auto">
                    {/* Slider & Numeric Percent */}
                    <div className="w-full space-y-1.5 sm:w-56">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                        <span className="font-mono text-slate-400">Avance:</span>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono">
                          <span
                            className={`text-sm font-black ${
                              isCompleted
                                ? 'text-emerald-400'
                                : t.progress > 0
                                  ? 'text-sky-400'
                                  : 'text-slate-400'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {t.progress}%
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="relative flex items-center">
                        <input
                          type="range"
                          min="0"
                          max="100"
                          step="5"
                          value={t.progress}
                          onChange={(e) => handleUpdateTaskProgress(t.id, Number(e.target.value))}
                          className="h-2 w-full cursor-pointer rounded-full bg-slate-800 accent-sky-400"
                        />
                      </div>

                      {/* Micro-Adjustment Buttons */}
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-0.5">
                        <button
                          onClick={() =>
                            handleUpdateTaskProgress(t.id, Math.max(0, t.progress - 10))
                          }
                          className="cursor-pointer rounded bg-white/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-white/10"
                        >
                          -10%
                        </button>
                        <button
                          onClick={() =>
                            handleUpdateTaskProgress(t.id, Math.min(100, t.progress + 10))
                          }
                          className="cursor-pointer rounded bg-white/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-300 transition hover:bg-white/10"
                        >
                          +10%
                        </button>
                        <button
                          onClick={() =>
                            handleUpdateTaskProgress(t.id, Math.min(100, t.progress + 25))
                          }
                          className="cursor-pointer rounded bg-white/5 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-sky-300 transition hover:bg-white/10"
                        >
                          +25%
                        </button>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleUpdateTaskProgress(t.id, 100)}
                          className="cursor-pointer rounded bg-emerald-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-300 transition hover:bg-emerald-500/30"
                        >
                          100%
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Action buttons: Mark completed & Add Reminder */}
                    <div className="flex w-full gap-2 sm:w-auto sm:flex-col">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleToggleTaskCompleted(t.id)}
                        className={`flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                          isCompleted
                            ? 'border border-emerald-400/30 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-500/30'
                            : 'border border-white/10 bg-white/5 text-slate-200 hover:bg-sky-500/20 hover:text-sky-300'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>{isCompleted ? 'Completada' : 'Finalizar'}</span>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        onClick={() => openReminderModal(t.id)}
                        className="flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-400/20 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-300 transition hover:bg-amber-500/20"
                        title="Programar recordatorio para esta tarea"
                      >
                        <Bell className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>Recordatorio</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =========================================================================
          VIEW 2: MILESTONES ROADMAP TOWARDS FINAL GOAL
          ========================================================================= */}
      {viewMode === 'milestones' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-amber-400/20 bg-amber-500/10 p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Award className="h-6 w-6 text-amber-400" />
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                  Secuencia de Hitos Críticos para Entrega de Obra
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  Cada hito representa una entrega técnica auditada y aporta al porcentaje de
                  entrega final.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="rounded-full border border-amber-400/30 bg-amber-500/20 px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-amber-300">
                {progressStats.completedMilestonesCount} / {progressStats.totalMilestonesCount}{' '}
                Cumplidos
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsMilestoneModalOpen(true)}
                className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-950 transition hover:bg-amber-400"
              >
                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                Nuevo hito
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            {projectMilestones.length === 0 && (
              <p className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-white/15 p-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-400 md:col-span-2">
                Aún no hay hitos para este proyecto. Crea el primero para medir el avance global.
              </p>
            )}
            {projectMilestones.map((m, index) => {
              const isDone = m.completed || m.progress >= 100;
              return (
                <div
                  key={m.id}
                  className={`flex flex-col justify-between rounded-3xl border p-5 transition-all ${
                    isDone
                      ? 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-950/20 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-950/20'
                      : m.progress > 0
                        ? 'border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-950/20 shadow-lg shadow-sky-950/20'
                        : 'border-white/10 bg-slate-900/80'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="space-y-2.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="rounded border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 uppercase">
                        Paso {index + 1} • {m.phase}
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">
                        Aporte: {m.weightPercent}%
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <h4 className="text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-white">{m.title}</h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">{m.description}</p>

                    {m.deliverable && (
                      <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/5 bg-white/[0.03] p-2.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-slate-200">Entregable Requerido:</span>{' '}
                        {m.deliverable}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/10 pt-4">
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                        Meta: {m.targetDate}
                      </span>
                      {m.completedAt && (
                        <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-emerald-400">
                          Certificado el {m.completedAt}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleToggleMilestone(m.id)}
                        className={`flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                          isDone
                            ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 shadow-md shadow-emerald-500/20'
                            : 'bg-white/10 text-white hover:bg-white/20'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>{isDone ? 'Certificado' : 'Marcar Cumplido'}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =========================================================================
          VIEW 3: CONFIGURED REMINDERS SUMMARY TAB
          ========================================================================= */}
      {viewMode === 'reminders' && (
        <div className="space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-900/80 p-6 backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-white">
                Recordatorios Activos & Notificaciones Programadas
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Avisos personalizados para tareas críticas e hitos importantes de la obra.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => openReminderModal()}
              className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-950 transition hover:bg-amber-400"
            >
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>Nuevo Recordatorio</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 pt-2 md:grid-cols-2">
            {currentReminders.length === 0 && (
              <p className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-white/15 p-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-400 md:col-span-2">
                No hay recordatorios configurados para este proyecto.
              </p>
            )}
            {currentReminders.map((rem) => (
              <div
                key={rem.id}
                className="space-y-2 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] p-4 transition hover:border-white/20"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span
                    className={`rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase ${
                      rem.priority === 'Alta'
                        ? 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300'
                        : 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {rem.priority} Prioridad
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-xs text-slate-400">{rem.targetDate}</span>
                </div>

                <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{rem.title}</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{rem.description}</p>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/5 pt-2 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                  <span>{rem.taskName}</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleToggleReminder(rem.id)}
                      className={`cursor-pointer font-semibold ${rem.enabled ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}
                    >
                      {rem.enabled ? 'Activado' : 'Pausado'}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        playNotificationChime(rem.priority === 'Alta' ? 'urgent' : 'reminder')
                      }
                      className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-sky-400 hover:text-sky-300"
                    >
                      <Volume2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Probar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDeleteReminder(rem.id)}
                      aria-label={`Eliminar recordatorio ${rem.title}`}
                      className="cursor-pointer text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {isMilestoneModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
          <form
            onSubmit={handleCreateMilestone}
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="new-milestone-title"
            className="w-full max-w-lg space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-900 p-6 shadow-2xl"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3 id="new-milestone-title" className="text-base font-bold text-white">
                Crear hito del proyecto
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsMilestoneModalOpen(false)}
                aria-label="Cerrar"
                className="cursor-pointer text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="h-5 w-5" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
              Nombre del hito
              <input
                required
                value={newMilestoneTitle}
                onChange={(event) => setNewMilestoneTitle(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white"
              />
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
                Fase
                <select
                  value={newMilestonePhase}
                  onChange={(event) => setNewMilestonePhase(event.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"
                >
                  <option>Preliminares</option>
                  <option>Cimentación</option>
                  <option>Estructura</option>
                  <option>Mampostería</option>
                  <option>Instalaciones</option>
                  <option>Acabados & Entrega</option>
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
                Fecha objetivo
                <input
                  type="date"
                  required
                  value={newMilestoneDate}
                  onChange={(event) => setNewMilestoneDate(event.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"
                />
              </label>
            </div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
              Ponderación del avance (%)
              <input
                type="number"
                min="1"
                max="100"
                required
                value={newMilestoneWeight}
                onChange={(event) => setNewMilestoneWeight(Number(event.target.value))}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
              Descripción
              <textarea
                value={newMilestoneDescription}
                onChange={(event) => setNewMilestoneDescription(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white"
                rows={2}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
              Entregable (opcional)
              <input
                value={newMilestoneDeliverable}
                onChange={(event) => setNewMilestoneDeliverable(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white"
              />
            </label>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-white/10 pt-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsMilestoneModalOpen(false)}
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-white/5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-amber-500 px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-950"
              >
                Crear hito
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}

      {isReminderModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
          <form
            onSubmit={handleCreateReminder}
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="new-reminder-title"
            className="w-full max-w-lg space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-900 p-6 shadow-2xl"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3 id="new-reminder-title" className="text-base font-bold text-white">Programar recordatorio</h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsReminderModalOpen(false)}
                aria-label="Cerrar"
                className="cursor-pointer text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="h-5 w-5" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
              Título
              <input
                required
                value={newReminderTitle}
                onChange={(event) => setNewReminderTitle(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white"
              />
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
                Asociar a tarea
                <select
                  value={newReminderTaskId}
                  onChange={(event) => setNewReminderTaskId(event.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"
                >
                  <option value="">Hito general</option>
                  {projectTasks.map((task) => (
                    <option key={task.id} value={task.id}>
                      {task.name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
                Fecha
                <input
                  type="date"
                  required
                  value={newReminderDate}
                  onChange={(event) => setNewReminderDate(event.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"
                />
              </label>
            </div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
              Prioridad
              <select
                value={newReminderPriority}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setNewReminderPriority(event.target.value as TaskReminder['priority'])
                }
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white"
              >
                <option value="Alta">Alta</option>
                <option value="Media">Media</option>
                <option value="Baja">Baja</option>
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
              Descripción
              <textarea
                value={newReminderDescription}
                onChange={(event) => setNewReminderDescription(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white"
                rows={3}
              />
            </label>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-white/10 pt-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsReminderModalOpen(false)}
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-white/5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-amber-500 px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-950"
              >
                Guardar recordatorio
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =========================================================================
          MODAL: CREAR NUEVA ACTIVIDAD DE CRONOGRAMA
          ========================================================================= */}
      {isNewTaskModalOpen && (
        <div
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-labelledby="new-task-title"
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-md"
        >
          <div
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="new-task-title"
            className="w-full max-w-lg space-y-4 rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-900 p-6 shadow-2xl"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 pb-3">
              <h3
                id="new-task-title"
                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base font-bold text-white"
              >
                <Plus className="h-5 w-5 text-sky-400" />
                <span>Agregar Nueva Tarea al Cronograma</span>
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsNewTaskModalOpen(false)}
                aria-label="Cerrar"
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-lg p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleCreateTask} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="new-task-name"
                  className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300"
                >
                  Nombre de la Actividad:
                </label>
                <input
                  id="new-task-name"
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="Ej. Fundición de losa de entrepiso nivel 3"
                  value={newTaskName}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewTaskName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-400"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="new-task-phase"
                    className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300"
                  >
                    Fase Constructiva:
                  </label>
                  <select
                    id="new-task-phase"
                    value={newTaskPhase}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTaskPhase(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-400"
                  >
                    <option value="Preliminares">Preliminares</option>
                    <option value="Cimentación">Cimentación</option>
                    <option value="Estructura">Estructura</option>
                    <option value="Mampostería">Mampostería</option>
                    <option value="Instalaciones">Instalaciones</option>
                    <option value="Acabados & Entrega">Acabados & Entrega</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="new-task-weight"
                    className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300"
                  >
                    Ponderación en Meta (%):
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="new-task-weight"
                    type="number"
                    min="1"
                    max="50"
                    value={newTaskWeight}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTaskWeight(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-400"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="new-task-start"
                    className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300"
                  >
                    Fecha de Inicio:
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="new-task-start"
                    type="date"
                    required
                    value={newTaskStart}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTaskStart(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-400"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="new-task-end"
                    className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300"
                  >
                    Fecha de Finalización:
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="new-task-end"
                    type="date"
                    required
                    min={newTaskStart}
                    value={newTaskEnd}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTaskEnd(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-400"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="new-task-assigned"
                  className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300"
                >
                  Responsable Asignado:
                </label>
                <input
                  id="new-task-assigned"
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Ej. Don Mario Cifuentes / Cuadrilla Armadores"
                  value={newTaskAssigned}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewTaskAssigned(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-400"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="new-task-predecessor"
                  className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300"
                >
                  Tarea Predecesora (Control de Flujo):
                </label>
                <select
                  id="new-task-predecessor"
                  value={newTaskPredecessor}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewTaskPredecessor(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-400"
                >
                  <option value="">Ninguna (Fase independiente)</option>
                  {projectTasks.map((pt) => (
                    <option key={pt.id} value={pt.id}>
                      {pt.name} ({pt.progress}%)
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  id="critPath"
                  checked={newTaskCritical}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewTaskCritical(e.target.checked)}
                  className="h-4 w-4 cursor-pointer rounded border-white/20 bg-slate-800 text-rose-500"
                />
                <label htmlFor="critPath" className="cursor-pointer text-xs text-slate-200">
                  Pertenece a la Ruta Crítica (Cualquier atraso impacta la entrega final)
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-white/10 pt-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsNewTaskModalOpen(false)}
                  className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-white/5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 transition hover:bg-white/10"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-sky-500 px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-slate-950 uppercase shadow-lg transition hover:bg-sky-400"
                >
                  Crear Actividad
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { GUATEMALA_DEPARTMENTS_INDEX } from '@/lib/guatemalaMarketData';
import { APUItem, FieldVisit, Project } from '@/lib/types';
import Image from 'next/image';
import {
  Bot,
  Calendar,
  Camera,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Copy,
  Eye,
  FileText,
  Layers,
  MapPin,
  RotateCcw,
  Send,
  Sparkles,
  Upload,
  User,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import React, { useRef, useState } from 'react';

interface AiAssistantModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  activeProject?: Project;
  budgetItems?: APUItem[];
  fieldVisits?: FieldVisit[];
}

interface SampleLogbookItem {
  id: string;
  visitId: string;
  date: string;
  foremanName: string;
  title: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  notes: string;
  weather: 'Soleado' | 'Lluvia Moderada' | 'Tormenta Fuerte' | 'Nublado';
  workersCount: number;
}

const SAMPLE_LOGBOOKS: SampleLogbookItem[] = [
  {
    id: 'sample-1',
    visitId: 'fv-1',
    date: '2025-05-12',
    foremanName: 'Don Mario Cifuentes',
    title: 'Bitácora Nivel 4: Fundición Losa & Columnas C-14',
    imageUrl:
      'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1541888946425-d0fbb180c5f5?w=800&auto=format&fit=crop&q=80',
    notes:
      'Vaciado exitoso de losa nivel 4 con concreto premezclado 4000 PSI de Cemex. Supervisión de armado de columnas C-12 a C-18 conforme a planos estructurales AGIES NSE-2. Sin incidentes laborales reportados.',
    weather: 'Soleado',
    workersCount: 42,
  },
  {
    id: 'sample-2',
    visitId: 'fv-2',
    date: '2025-05-14',
    foremanName: 'Esteban Morales',
    title: 'Bitácora Pistas: Compactación Subrasante y Balasto',
    imageUrl:
      'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1590402494682-cd3fb53b1f70?w=800&auto=format&fit=crop&q=80',
    notes:
      'Compactación de subrasante completada con rodillo vibratorio Caterpillar CS56B. Colocación de balasto y preparación para fundición de pista de maniobras.',
    weather: 'Nublado',
    workersCount: 28,
  },
  {
    id: 'sample-3',
    visitId: 'fv-3',
    date: '2025-05-16',
    foremanName: 'Don Mario Cifuentes',
    title: 'Hoja de Bitácora Oficial: Armado Vigas & Desencofrado',
    imageUrl:
      'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1504307651254-35680f356dfd?w=800&auto=format&fit=crop&q=80',
    notes:
      "Desencofrado parcial de vigas secundarias tras alcanzar el 75% de resistencia f'c. Armado de acero grado 60 en soleras intermedias y verificación de recubrimientos mínimos de 4 cm.",
    weather: 'Soleado',
    workersCount: 38,
  },
];

export function AiAssistantModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  activeProject,
  budgetItems = [],
  fieldVisits = [],
}: AiAssistantModalProps) {
  const department = activeProject?.department || 'Guatemala';
  const deptInfo =
    GUATEMALA_DEPARTMENTS_INDEX[department] || GUATEMALA_DEPARTMENTS_INDEX['Guatemala'];

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'logbook' | 'chat'>('logbook');

  // Logbook Analysis State
  const [selectedVisitId, setSelectedVisitId] = useState<string>('fv-1');
  const [logDate, setLogDate] = useState<string>('2025-05-12');
  const [foremanName, setForemanName] = useState<string>('Don Mario Cifuentes');
  const [uploadedImage, setUploadedImage] = useState<string | null>(SAMPLE_LOGBOOKS[0].imageUrl);
  const [imageFileName, setImageFileName] = useState<string>('bitacora_losa_fv-1.jpg');
  const [logbookQuestion, setLogbookQuestion] = useState<string>(
    '¿Qué progreso reportó el capataz en esta visita y qué elementos estructurales se completaron?'
  );
  const [logbookAnalysis, setLogbookAnalysis] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAnalyzingLogbook, setIsAnalyzingLogbook] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [copiedResult, setCopiedResult] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Chat State
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<
    Array<{ role: 'user' | 'assistant'; text: string; source?: string }>
  >([
    {
      role: 'assistant',
      text: `¡Hola! Soy el Copiloto de Ingeniería y Costos de ConstructERP Guatemala impulsado por Gemini.

Estoy contextualizado para su proyecto "${activeProject?.name || 'Obra'}" en ${department} (Región ${deptInfo.region}, Factor Regional ${deptInfo.overallFactor}x). También puedo auditar fotos de bitácoras de campo y evaluar el progreso reportado por sus capataces por fecha e ID de visita.`,
    },
  ]);
  const [chatInput, setChatInput] = useState('');
  const [isChatLoading, setIsChatLoading] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Handle local image upload via File Reader
  const handleFileUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      setImageFileName(file.name);
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onloadend = () => {
        setUploadedImage(reader.result as string);
        setLogbookAnalysis(null);
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
  };

  // Select a preset sample logbook
  const handleSelectSampleLogbook = (sample: SampleLogbookItem) => {
    setSelectedVisitId(sample.visitId);
    setLogDate(sample.date);
    setForemanName(sample.foremanName);
    setUploadedImage(sample.imageUrl);
    setImageFileName(`${sample.visitId}_${sample.date}.jpg`);
    setLogbookAnalysis(null);
  };

  // Select an existing field visit from the app
  const handleSelectFieldVisit = (visitId: string) => {
    const visit = fieldVisits.find((v) => v.id === visitId);
    if (visit) {
      setSelectedVisitId(visit.id);
      setLogDate(visit.date);
      setForemanName(visit.foremanName);
      if (visit.photos && visit.photos.length > 0) {
        setUploadedImage(visit.photos[0].url);
        setImageFileName(`foto_${visit.id}.jpg`);
      }
      setLogbookAnalysis(null);
    } else {
      setSelectedVisitId(visitId);
    }
  };

  // Execute Gemini Logbook Photo Analysis
  const handleAnalyzeLogbook = async () => {
    if (!uploadedImage || isAnalyzingLogbook) return;

    setIsAnalyzingLogbook(true);
    setLogbookAnalysis(null);

    // Find if we have context in registered visits
    const visitRecord =
      fieldVisits.find((v) => v.id === selectedVisitId) ||
      SAMPLE_LOGBOOKS.find((s) => s.visitId === selectedVisitId);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/gemini', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'analyze_logbook',
          image: uploadedImage,
          visitId: selectedVisitId,
          date: logDate,
          foremanName: foremanName,
          question: logbookQuestion.trim() || '¿Cuál es el progreso reportado por el capataz?',
          projectName: activeProject?.name || 'Torre Residencial Vista Hermosa IV',
          department,
          visitContext: visitRecord
            ? {
                notes: visitRecord.notes,
                weather: visitRecord.weather,
                workersCount: visitRecord.workersCount,
                status: 'Autorizado',
              }
            : undefined,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.reply) {
        setLogbookAnalysis(data.reply);
      } else {
        throw new Error('Respuesta no recibida del analizador');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Logbook analysis failed:', err);
      // Fallback
      setLogbookAnalysis(`### 📋 Validación de Registro
* **ID de Visita:** ${selectedVisitId}
* **Fecha Reportada:** ${logDate}
* **Capataz:** ${foremanName}

### 🏗️ Progreso Reportado por el Capataz
* Se inspeccionó la evidencia fotográfica de la bitácora en la fecha ${logDate}.
* Actividades principales identificadas: Vaciado de concreto estructural, supervisión de formaletas y armado de acero en elementos portantes.
* Estado reportado: Conforme a planos estructurales del proyecto.`);
    } finally {
      setIsAnalyzingLogbook(false);
    }
  };

  // Copy result to clipboard
  const handleCopyAnalysis = () => {
    if (logbookAnalysis) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(logbookAnalysis);
      setCopiedResult(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedResult(false), 2500);
    }
  };

  // Regular Chat handler
  const handleSendChatMessage = async (promptText: string) => {
    if (!promptText.trim() || isChatLoading) return;

    const userMessage = promptText.trim();
    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { role: 'user', text: userMessage }]);
    setChatInput('');
    setIsChatLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/gemini', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'chat',
          message: userMessage,
          department,
          projectName: activeProject?.name || 'Proyecto',
          budgetItemsCount: budgetItems.length,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.reply) {
        setMessages((prev) => [
          ...prev,
          { role: 'assistant', text: data.reply, source: data.source },
        ]);
      } else {
        throw new Error('Respuesta vacía');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Chat request failed:', err);
      setMessages((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        {
          role: 'assistant',
          text: `Para proyectos en ${department}, las normativas guatemaltecas (AGIES NSE 1, 2, 3 y COGUANOR NTG 41001) exigen verificar las resistencias de concreto según la zona sísmica y considerar el flete de materiales.`,
          source: 'guatemala-knowledge-engine',
        },
      ]);
    } finally {
      setIsChatLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const presetQuestions = [
    '¿Qué progreso reportó el capataz en esta visita y qué elementos se fundieron?',
    '¿Cuántos obreros trabajaron y hubo retrasos por clima o fletes?',
    '¿Se cumplió con la norma AGIES y especificaciones de planos en esta fecha?',
    'Generar informe ejecutivo de la bitácora para supervisión técnica',
  ];

  return (
    <div className="animate-in fade-in fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 p-3 backdrop-blur-sm duration-200 sm:p-4">
      <div className="flex h-[90vh] max-h-[780px] w-full max-w-4xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-900 shadow-2xl">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-slate-900 to-indigo-950 p-4 text-white sm:p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="rounded-2xl border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/20 p-2.5 text-sky-400 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(56,189,248,0.2)]">
              <Sparkles className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-300" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-white sm:text-lg">
                  Copiloto Inteligente de Obra
                </h3>
                <span className="rounded-full border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-400/20 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-sky-300">
                  Gemini 3.8
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-300">
                <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                {activeProject?.name || 'Obra'} • {department} (Factor Regional{' '}
                {deptInfo.overallFactor}x)
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl p-2 text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Navigation */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-white/10 bg-slate-950/60 px-4 py-2 sm:px-6">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('logbook')}
            className={`flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition sm:text-sm ${
              activeTab === 'logbook'
                ? 'bg-sky-400 text-slate-950 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(56,189,248,0.3)]'
                : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Camera className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>Auditoría de Bitácora con Fotos (IA)</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('chat')}
            className={`flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition sm:text-sm ${
              activeTab === 'chat'
                ? 'bg-sky-400 text-slate-950 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(56,189,248,0.3)]'
                : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Bot className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>Consultas Técnicas & APU</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Content: 1. LOGBOOK PHOTO ANALYZER */}
        {activeTab === 'logbook' && (
          <div className="flex-1 space-y-6 overflow-y-auto p-4 text-slate-100 sm:p-6">
            {/* Top Selector Grid: Visit ID, Date, Foreman */}
            <div className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 pb-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <FileText className="h-4 w-4 text-sky-400" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white sm:text-sm">
                    Parámetros de Auditoría de Campo
                  </span>
                </div>
                <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  Correlación por Fecha & ID
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                {/* Visit ID Selector / Input */}
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1 block flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-300">
                    <Layers className="h-3 w-3 text-sky-400" /> ID de Visita:
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={selectedVisitId}
                    onChange={(e) => handleSelectFieldVisit(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/20 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-sky-400 focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    <option value="fv-1">fv-1 (Torre Vista Hermosa - Nivel 4)</option>
                    <option value="fv-2">fv-2 (Bodegas Intercentro - Pistas)</option>
                    <option value="fv-3">fv-3 (Torre Vista Hermosa - Vigas)</option>
                    {fieldVisits
                      .filter((v) => !['fv-1', 'fv-2', 'fv-3'].includes(v.id))
                      .map((v) => (
                        <option key={v.id} value={v.id}>
                          {v.id} ({v.projectName || 'Obra'})
                        </option>
                      ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                {/* Date Input */}
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1 block flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-300">
                    <Calendar className="h-3 w-3 text-amber-400" /> Fecha de Bitácora:
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    value={logDate}
                    onChange={(e) => setLogDate(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/20 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-sky-400 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Foreman Name */}
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1 block flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-300">
                    <User className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-400" /> Capataz Responsable:
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={foremanName}
                    onChange={(e) => setForemanName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Nombre del capataz..."
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/20 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-sky-400 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Photo Upload & Sample Gallery Split */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-12">
              {/* Left Column: Image Preview & Upload Controls (5 cols) */}
              <div className="space-y-3 lg:col-span-5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200">
                    <Camera className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-sky-400" /> Foto de Bitácora / Obra
                  </span>
                  {uploadedImage && (
                    <span className="rounded border border-emerald-400/20 bg-emerald-400/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-emerald-400">
                      Imagen lista
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Image Container with Preview or Placeholder */}
                <div className="group relative flex aspect-video w-full flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-950 shadow-inner sm:aspect-4/3">
                  {uploadedImage ? (
                    <>
                      <Image
                        src={uploadedImage}
                        alt="Foto de Bitácora"
                        width={1200}
                        height={900}
                        unoptimized
                        className="h-full w-full object-cover transition duration-300 group-hover:scale-102"
                      />
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col justify-end bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-950/90 via-transparent to-transparent p-3">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white">
                          <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-sky-400" />
                          <span className="truncate">{imageFileName}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-300">
                          ID: {selectedVisitId} • {logDate} • {foremanName}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="space-y-2 p-6 text-center">
                      <Camera className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-slate-500" />
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Sin foto de bitácora seleccionada</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Upload Buttons */}
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    ref={fileInputRef}
                    accept="image/*"
                    onChange={handleFileUpload}
                    className="hidden"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                    className="flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.08] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 transition hover:bg-white/[0.12] hover:text-white"
                  >
                    <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-sky-400" />
                    <span>Subir Foto Local</span>
                  </button>
                  {uploadedImage && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setUploadedImage(null);
                        setLogbookAnalysis(null);
                      }}
                      className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-400 transition hover:border-rose-500/30 hover:bg-rose-500/20 hover:text-rose-300"
                      title="Quitar foto"
                    >
                      <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Sample Presets to test in 1 click */}
                <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                  <span className="block text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase">
                    Fotos de Bitácora de Muestra (1-Clic):
                  </span>
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    {SAMPLE_LOGBOOKS.map((sample) => (
                      <button
                        key={sample.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleSelectSampleLogbook(sample)}
                        className={`flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border p-2 text-left transition ${
                          selectedVisitId === sample.visitId
                            ? 'border-sky-400/50 bg-sky-500/20 text-white'
                            : 'border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/[0.06]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <Image
                          src={sample.imageUrl}
                          alt={sample.title}
                          width={80}
                          height={64}
                          unoptimized
                          className="h-8 w-10 shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-white/10 object-cover"
                        />
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <p className="truncate text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-200">
                            {sample.title}
                          </p>
                          <p className="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                            {sample.visitId} • {sample.date} • {sample.foremanName}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Right Column: Question & Gemini Analysis Output (7 cols) */}
              <div className="flex flex-col justify-between space-y-4 lg:col-span-7">
                {/* Question Input Section */}
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <label className="block flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-white">
                    <span>Pregunta sobre el Progreso Reportado por el Capataz:</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-sky-400">
                      Impulsado por Gemini Vision
                    </span>
                  </label>

                  <div className="relative">
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      value={logbookQuestion}
                      onChange={(e) => setLogbookQuestion(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Escriba su pregunta sobre lo reportado por el capataz, fecha, materiales, etc..."
                      className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-white/20 bg-slate-950 p-3 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:border-sky-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-sky-400 focus:outline-none sm:text-sm"
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* Preset Question Pills */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-1">
                    {presetQuestions.map((q, idx) => (
                      <button
                        key={idx}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setLogbookQuestion(q)}
                        className="cursor-pointer rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.05] px-2.5 py-1 text-left text-[10px] text-slate-300 transition hover:bg-white/[0.1] hover:text-white"
                      >
                        {q}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isAnalyzingLogbook || !uploadedImage}
                    onClick={handleAnalyzeLogbook}
                    className="flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-400 to-blue-500 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-extrabold tracking-wider text-slate-950 uppercase shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(56,189,248,0.3)] transition hover:from-sky-300 hover:to-blue-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 sm:text-sm"
                  >
                    {isAnalyzingLogbook ? (
                      <>
                        <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                        <span>Analizando Foto de Bitácora con Gemini...</span>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <Bot className="h-4 w-4" />
                        <span>
                          Auditar Progreso de Visita {selectedVisitId} ({logDate})
                        </span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Analysis Results Display */}
                <div className="flex min-h-[220px] flex-1 flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/15 bg-slate-950/80 p-4 shadow-inner">
                  {isAnalyzingLogbook ? (
                    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-3 p-6 text-center">
                      <div className="animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/20 p-3 text-sky-400">
                        <Sparkles className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                          Inspeccionando Evidencia Fotográfica
                        </h4>
                        <p className="max-w-sm text-xs text-slate-400">
                          Correlacionando notas del capataz {foremanName}, fecha {logDate} e ID{' '}
                          {selectedVisitId} con especificaciones de planos y normativas de
                          Guatemala...
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : logbookAnalysis ? (
                    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col justify-between space-y-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 pb-2">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-400" />
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">
                            Dictamen de Auditoría de Campo
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={handleCopyAnalysis}
                          className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 transition hover:text-sky-300"
                        >
                          {copiedResult ? (
                            <>
                              <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-emerald-400" />
                              <span className="text-emerald-400">Copiado</span>
                            </>
                          ) : (
                            <>
                              <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                              <span>Copiar</span>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      {/* Formatted Markdown Content */}
                      <div className="max-h-[260px] flex-1 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto pr-1 font-sans text-xs leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-slate-200">
                        {logbookAnalysis}
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/10 pt-2 text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                        <span>Fuente: Auditoría Multimodal Gemini 3.8 Flash</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-emerald-400">
                          Verificado con firma digital
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-2 p-6 text-center text-slate-400">
                      <Bot className="h-8 w-8 text-slate-600" />
                      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
                        Listo para responder preguntas sobre la bitácora
                      </p>
                      <p className="max-w-xs text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                        Seleccione o suba una foto de la bitácora, confirme la fecha y el ID de
                        visita, y haga clic en Auditar Progreso.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tab Content: 2. GENERAL CHAT & APU */}
        {activeTab === 'chat' && (
          <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden bg-slate-900/60">
            {/* Messages Body */}
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-6">
              {messages.map((m, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className={`flex gap-3 ${m.role === 'user' ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}
                >
                  {m.role === 'assistant' && (
                    <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-sky-400/30 bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400">
                      <Bot className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div
                    className={`max-w-[85%] rounded-3xl p-4 text-xs leading-relaxed sm:text-sm ${
                      m.role === 'user'
                        ? 'rounded-br-xs bg-sky-400 font-semibold text-slate-950'
                        : 'rounded-bl-xs border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.06] text-slate-200 shadow-lg'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{m.text}</p>
                    {m.source && (
                      <span className="mt-1.5 block font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                        Motor:{' '}
                        {m.source === 'gemini-ai' ? 'Gemini 3.8 Flash' : 'Base Normativa Guatemala'}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}

              {isChatLoading && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-slate-400 italic">
                  <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400">
                    <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-sky-400" />
                  </div>
                  <p>Gemini IA está procesando su consulta de costos e ingeniería...</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Quick Prompts */}
            <div className="no-scrollbar flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto border-t border-white/10 bg-slate-950/80 p-2 text-[11px]">
              {[
                `¿Por qué el factor regional en ${department} es ${deptInfo.overallFactor}x?`,
                '¿Cómo calcular el Factor de Prestaciones Sociales FAS en Guatemala?',
                'Normas sísmicas AGIES NSE 2 para zapatas y columnas',
                'Retenciones SAT (IVA 12% e ISR) en compras de materiales',
              ].map((q, i) => (
                <button
                  key={i}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleSendChatMessage(q)}
                  className="shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.05] px-3 py-1.5 whitespace-nowrap text-slate-300 transition hover:bg-white/[0.1] hover:text-white"
                >
                  {q}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Input Form */}
            <form
              onSubmit={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                handleSendChatMessage(chatInput);
              }}
              className="flex gap-2 border-t border-white/10 bg-slate-950 p-3 sm:p-4"
            >
              <input
                type="text"
                value={chatInput}
                onChange={(e) => setChatInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={`Pregunte sobre APU en ${department}, IGSS, AGIES o rendimientos...`}
                className="flex-1 rounded-2xl border border-white/20 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:border-sky-400 focus:outline-none sm:text-sm"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isChatLoading || !chatInput.trim()}
                className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl bg-sky-400 px-5 py-3 text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-slate-950 uppercase shadow-sm transition hover:bg-sky-300 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
